<script id="ethvelle-v85-final-interactions-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Reposition the whole transport group by the difference between the actual blue playhead
  // centre and the Play/Pause button centre, so the Play/Pause control is always exact-centre.
  function alignNormalTransport(){
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');
    const cluster=row&&row.querySelector('.transport-cluster');
    const play=row&&row.querySelector('#playBtn');
    if(!row||!cluster||!play||!row.getClientRects().length)return;
    const line=$('playhead'),lr=line&&line.getBoundingClientRect();
    const rr=row.getBoundingClientRect(),pr=play.getBoundingClientRect();
    if(!rr.width||!pr.width)return;
    const target=lr&&lr.width>0?lr.left+lr.width/2:rr.left+rr.width/2;
    const actual=pr.left+pr.width/2;
    const shift=parseFloat(cluster.style.getPropertyValue('--ev-play-center-shift'))||0;
    const next=shift+(target-actual);
    if(Math.abs(next-shift)>0.35)cluster.style.setProperty('--ev-play-center-shift',next.toFixed(2)+'px');
  }
  let alignRaf=0;
  function scheduleTransportAlign(){if(alignRaf)return;alignRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{alignRaf=0;alignNormalTransport();});}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scheduleTransportAlign,{once:true});
  else scheduleTransportAlign();
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleTransportAlign,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleTransportAlign,{passive:true});
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const ro=new ResizeObserver(scheduleTransportAlign);
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');if(row)ro.observe(row);
    // Do not observe the entire timeline scroller: lane-height changes during clip drag
    // would otherwise schedule layout measurements for the transport on every move frame.
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const screen=$('screen-editor');if(screen){const mo=new MutationObserver(scheduleTransportAlign);mo.observe(screen,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style']});}
  }

  // Ensure object keyframe detection follows the visible diamond width (including Background
  // and Text) and keep the bottom Key action available for every object-keyframe layer.
  let objectToolSyncRaf=0;
  function scheduleObjectKeyToolSync(){
    if(objectToolSyncRaf)return;
    objectToolSyncRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{objectToolSyncRaf=0;stabilizeObjectKeyTool();});
  }
  function stabilizeObjectKeyTool(){
    const id=(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null)?'':String(selectedClip);
    const edit=$('clipTextEditTool');
    if(!id){if(edit)edit.style.display='none';return;}
    let target=null;
    if(typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function')target=window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id);
    if(!target){
      const bgList=(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds:(Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds:[]);
      const ovList=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
      target=bgList.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id)||ovList.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id)||null;
    }
    if(!target){if(edit)edit.style.display='none';return;}
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip'),key=$('clipKeyTool');
    // Use normal inline styles, not !important. syncBottomToolsMode runs first on every
    // selection and must remain able to hide/reset these controls when switching layers.
    if(row&&(row.style.getPropertyValue('display')!=='flex'||row.style.getPropertyPriority('display')!=='important'))row.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
    if(key)key.style.display='flex';
    if(edit)edit.style.display=target.type==='text'?'flex':'none';
    ['clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'].forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.style.display='none';});
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv85){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);scheduleObjectKeyToolSync();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv85=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv85){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleObjectKeyToolSync();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv85=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden)scheduleTransportAlign();});
  window.addEventListener('load',()=>{scheduleTransportAlign();stabilizeObjectKeyTool();},{once:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v86-live-lane-layout-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(typeof window.packIntoLanes!=='function'||typeof window.overlayRowHeightForLanes!=='function')return;
  window.applyOverlayLanes=function(groupKey){
    const row=document.querySelector(`.overlay-track-row[data-group="${groupKey}"]`);
    if(!row)return;
    const track=row.querySelector('.layer-track');
    if(!track)return;
    const source=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    const clips=source.filter(o=>groupKey==='audio'?o.type==='audio':(groupKey==='text'?o.type==='text':o.type!=='audio'&&o.type!=='text'));
    const lanes=window.packIntoLanes(clips);
    const laneCount=Math.max(1,lanes.length);
    const signature=lanes.map(lane=>lane.map(o=>String(o.id)).sort().join(',')).join('|');
    if(row.dataset.evV86LaneSignature===signature)return;

    row.dataset.evV86LaneSignature=signature;
    const rowH=window.overlayRowHeightForLanes(laneCount);
    const trackH=rowH;
    if(row.style.height!==(rowH+4)+'px')row.style.height=(rowH+4)+'px';
    if(row.style.minHeight!==(rowH+4)+'px')row.style.minHeight=(rowH+4)+'px';
    if(row.style.maxHeight!=='none')row.style.maxHeight='none';
    if(track.style.height!==trackH+'px')track.style.height=trackH+'px';
    if(track.style.minHeight!==trackH+'px')track.style.minHeight=trackH+'px';
    if(track.style.maxHeight!==trackH+'px')track.style.maxHeight=trackH+'px';

    const elements=track.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]');
    const byId=new Map();
    for(let i=0;i<elements.length;i++)byId.set(String(elements[i].dataset.overlayId),elements[i]);
    lanes.forEach((lane,laneIndex)=>{
      const top=window.overlayLaneTop(laneIndex)+'px';
      lane.forEach(clip=>{
        const el=byId.get(String(clip.id));
        if(el&&el.style.top!==top)el.style.top=top;
      });
    });
  };
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v86-keyframe-touch-guard-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  // A keyframe diamond is a separate tap target, never a clip-body drag/scrub target.
  // Stop its pointerdown in capture phase so parent clip handlers cannot arm a long-press.
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(marker)e.stopPropagation();
  },true);
  // Refit only; rotating the device must not call toggleCanvasZoomView. The existing v82
  // ResizeObserver recomputes canvas size while preserving the user's current zoom state.
  window.__ethvelleRefitZoomViewport=window.__ethvelleRefitZoomViewport||function(){
    const screen=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
    const wrap=screen&&screen.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');
    const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
    if(!screen||!wrap||!stage||!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const project=window.currentProject;
    const ratio=(project&&project.ratio)||'16:9';
    if(typeof window.setupCanvasStage==='function')window.setupCanvasStage(ratio);
    if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.renderFrame());
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v88-interaction-and-tools-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const getSelectedId=()=>typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':String(selectedClip);
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const ovFor=id=>{try{return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const bgFor=id=>{try{return (typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const isImageSource=src=>typeof src==='string'&&(/^(?:url\(|data:image\/|blob:|https?:\/\/)/i.test(src.trim()));
  const cropFractions=c=>{
    const v=c||{};let left=clamp(v.left,0,.45),right=clamp(v.right,0,.45),top=clamp(v.top,0,.45),bottom=clamp(v.bottom,0,.45);
    if(left+right>.8){const f=.8/(left+right);left*=f;right*=f;}
    if(top+bottom>.8){const f=.8/(top+bottom);top*=f;bottom*=f;}
    return {left,top,right,bottom};
  };
  window.ethvelleCropFractions=cropFractions;

  // Keep another clip from becoming selected while a held clip is being moved.
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv87Lock){
    const wrapped=function(which){
      const active=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .anim-clip.dragging,#timelineInner .overlay-clip.dragging,#timelineInner .track-clip.dragging,#timelineInner [data-ev-timeline-drag-active="1"]');
      let lock=String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId||'');
      if(!lock&&active){
        if(active.dataset.overlayId)lock=String(active.dataset.overlayId);
        else if(active.dataset.bgId)lock=String(active.dataset.bgId);
        else if(active.dataset.segIdx!=null)lock='seg'+active.dataset.segIdx;
        else if(active.id==='animClipEl')lock='main';
      }
      const playheadDrivenScrub=!!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&!active;
      if(lock&&String(which)!==lock&&!playheadDrivenScrub)return;
      return oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv87Lock=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  // Capture the clip identity at the start of the gesture. Pointer capture can then move
  // across neighbouring clips without allowing a hover/click side-effect to change selection.
  let selectionLockPointer=null;
  function clipRefFromTarget(target){
    const ov=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]');if(ov)return String(ov.dataset.overlayId);
    const bg=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.track-clip[data-bg-id]');if(bg)return String(bg.dataset.bgId);
    const anim=target&&target.closest&&target.closest('.anim-clip');
    if(anim){if(anim.id==='animClipEl')return 'main';if(/^seg\d+El$/.test(anim.id))return anim.id.replace(/El$/,'');if(anim.dataset.readonlyClipRef)return String(anim.dataset.readonlyClipRef);}
    return '';
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const ref=clipRefFromTarget(e.target);if(!ref)return;
    window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId=ref;selectionLockPointer=e.pointerId;
  },true);
  function releaseSelectionLock(e){
    if(selectionLockPointer==null||(e&&e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==selectionLockPointer))return;
    selectionLockPointer=null;
    window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId='';
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',releaseSelectionLock,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',releaseSelectionLock,true);
  document.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{if(clipRefFromTarget(e.target)){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}},true);

  // Reverse playback timestamp is shared by preview, metadata-ready and export paths.
  window.ethvelleVideoSourceTime=function(ov,t,duration){
    const speed=Math.max(.05,Number(ov&&ov.speed)||1);
    const max=Math.max(.05,(Number(duration)||Number(ov&&ov.sourceDuration)||Number(ov&&ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))-.02);
    const elapsed=Math.max(0,(Number(t)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))*speed);
    const span=Math.max(0,(Number(ov&&ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov&&ov.trimStart))*speed);
    const raw=ov&&ov.reversePlayback?(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+span-elapsed:(Number(ov&&ov.sourceOffset)||0)+elapsed;
    return clamp(raw,0,max);
  };
  window.toggleSelectedVideoReverse=function(){
    const id=getSelectedId(),ov=ovFor(id);
    if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select a video clip to reverse.',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});return;}
    ov.reversePlayback=!ov.reversePlayback;
    const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[id]:null;
    if(el){try{el.pause();el.muted=!!ov.reversePlayback;el.currentTime=window.ethvelleVideoSourceTime(ov,typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):0),el.duration);}catch(_e){}}
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast(ov.reversePlayback?'Reverse enabled':'Reverse disabled',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check,duration:1200});
    syncMediaTools();
  };

  // Add Reverse/Crop actions to the existing contextual layer toolbar, not as duplicate rows.
  function ensureMediaTools(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    if(!$('clipReverseTool')){
      const ref=$('clipTrimTool')||$('clipSpeedTool');
      const html=`<div class="tool-item" id="clipReverseTool" onclick="toggleSelectedVideoReverse()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M4 7v5h5M20 17v-5h-5"/><path d="M6.5 16A7 7 0 0 0 19 12M17.5 8A7 7 0 0 0 5 12"/></svg><span>Reverse</span></div>`;
      if(ref)ref.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',html);else row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);
    }
    if(!$('clipCropTool')){
      const ref=$('clipReverseTool')||$('clipTrimTool');
      const html=`<div class="tool-item" id="clipCropTool" onclick="openPanel('crop')"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M7 3v13a1 1 0 0 0 1 1h13"/><path d="M3 7h13a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v13"/><path d="M7 7h10v10"/></svg><span>Crop</span></div>`;
      if(ref)ref.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',html);else row.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',html);
    }
  }
  function syncMediaTools(){
    ensureMediaTools();
    const id=getSelectedId(),ov=ovFor(id),bg=bgFor(id);
    const reverse=$('clipReverseTool'),crop=$('clipCropTool');
    if(reverse)reverse.style.display=ov&&ov.type==='video'?'flex':'none';
    if(crop)crop.style.display=(ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='image'))||(bg&&isImageSource(bg.src))?'flex':'none';
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv87Media){window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.syncBottomToolsMode.__evv87Media=true;}
  const oldSelect2=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect2==='function'&&!oldSelect2.__evv87Media){window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const r=oldSelect2.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.selectClipForChrome.__evv87Media=true;}
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evv87Media){window.deselectAllClips=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.deselectAllClips.__evv87Media=true;}

  // Tick-ruler Transform panel. Existing slider IDs and updateTransform() API are preserved.
  function rulerRow(label,id,valueId,min,max,step,suffix){
    return `<div class="evtr-slider-row"><div class="evtr-slider-head"><span>${label}</span><span class="evtr-slider-value" id="${valueId}">0${suffix||''}</span></div><div class="evtr-ruler" data-evtr-ruler="${id}"><input type="range" id="${id}" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step||1}" value="0" oninput="updateTransform();ethvelleSyncTransformRulers()" aria-label="${label}"></div></div>`;
  }
  function transformBody(){return `<div class="evv-transform-slider-panel">
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Size</span><span class="transform-value" id="sizeVal">100%</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="sizeSlider" min="10" max="400" step="1" value="100" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Size">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Horizontal position</span><span class="transform-value" id="posXVal">0</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="posXSlider" min="-200" max="200" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Horizontal position">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Vertical position</span><span class="transform-value" id="posYVal">0</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="posYSlider" min="-200" max="200" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Vertical position">
    </div>
    <div class="transform-group">
      <div class="transform-label-row"><span>Rotation</span><span class="transform-value" id="rotVal">0°</span></div>
      <input type="range" class="transform-slider" id="rotSlider" min="-180" max="180" step="1" value="0" oninput="updateTransform()" aria-label="Rotation">
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="transform-reset-btn" onclick="resetTransform()">Reset transform</button>
  </div>`;}
  function syncTransformRulers(){
    ['posXSlider','posYSlider','sizeSlider','rotSlider'].forEach(id=>{
      const input=$(id),wrap=document.querySelector(`[data-evtr-ruler="${id}"]`);if(!input||!wrap)return;
      const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,value=Number(input.value)||0;
      const pct=max>min?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(value-min)/(max-min))):.5;
      const usable=Math.max(1,(wrap.clientWidth||1)-20);
      wrap.style.setProperty('--evtr-progress',pct.toFixed(4));
      wrap.style.setProperty('--evtr-shift',((.5-pct)*usable).toFixed(2)+'px');
    });
    const sv=$('sizeVal'),xv=$('posXVal'),yv=$('posYVal'),rv=$('rotVal');
    if(sv&&$('sizeSlider'))sv.textContent=$('sizeSlider').value+'%';
    if(xv&&$('posXSlider'))xv.textContent=$('posXSlider').value;
    if(yv&&$('posYSlider'))yv.textContent=$('posYSlider').value;
    if(rv&&$('rotSlider'))rv.textContent=$('rotSlider').value+'°';
  }
  window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers=syncTransformRulers;
  window.ethvelleTransformTab=function(name){
    document.querySelectorAll('#toolPanel .evtr-tab').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.evtrTab===name));
    document.querySelectorAll('#toolPanel .evtr-pane').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('active',el.dataset.evtrPane===name));
  };
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined'&&panelContent.transform)panelContent.transform.body=transformBody();
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv87Transform){
    window.openPanel=function(key){
      const r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform'){window.ethvelleTransformTab('position');requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(typeof window.syncTransformSliders==='function')window.syncTransformSliders();syncTransformRulers();});}
      if(key==='crop')requestAnimationFrame(syncCropPanel);
      return r;
    };
    window.openPanel.__evv87Transform=true;
  }
  const oldSyncTransform=window.syncTransformSliders;
  if(typeof oldSyncTransform==='function'&&!oldSyncTransform.__evv87Ruler){
    window.syncTransformSliders=function(){const r=oldSyncTransform.apply(this,arguments);syncTransformRulers();return r;};
    window.syncTransformSliders.__evv87Ruler=true;
  }
  const oldUpdateTransform=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof oldUpdateTransform==='function'&&!oldUpdateTransform.__evv87Ruler){
    window.updateTransform=function(){const r=oldUpdateTransform.apply(this,arguments);syncTransformRulers();return r;};
    window.updateTransform.__evv87Ruler=true;
  }

  // Crop editor data model: normalized fractions are applied in live preview and in export.
  function currentCropTarget(){const id=getSelectedId();const ov=ovFor(id),bg=bgFor(id);if(ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='image'))return {kind:'overlay',clip:ov};if(bg&&isImageSource(bg.src))return {kind:'background',clip:bg};return null;}
  function cropBody(){return `<div class="evcr-root">
    ${[['Left','left'],['Top','top'],['Right','right'],['Bottom','bottom']].map(([label,key])=>`<div class="evcr-row"><div class="evcr-head"><span>${label}</span><span id="evcr-${key}-value">0%</span></div><input type="range" id="evcr-${key}" min="0" max="45" step="1" value="0" oninput="ethvelleApplyCropFromControls()"></div>`).join('')}
    <div class="evcr-footer"><button type="button" class="evtr-reset" onclick="ethvelleResetCrop()">↶ Reset crop</button><button type="button" class="evtr-done" onclick="closePanel()" aria-label="Done">Apply</button></div></div>`;}
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined')panelContent.crop={title:'Crop',body:cropBody()};
  function getCropTarget(){return currentCropTarget();}
  function syncCropPanel(){
    const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;
    const c=cropFractions(target.clip.crop);
    ['left','top','right','bottom'].forEach(k=>{const input=$('evcr-'+k),value=$('evcr-'+k+'-value');if(input)input.value=Math.round(c[k]*100);if(value)value.textContent=Math.round(c[k]*100)+'%';});
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCropFromControls=function(){
    const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;
    const c=cropFractions({left:Number($('evcr-left')&&$('evcr-left').value)/100,top:Number($('evcr-top')&&$('evcr-top').value)/100,right:Number($('evcr-right')&&$('evcr-right').value)/100,bottom:Number($('evcr-bottom')&&$('evcr-bottom').value)/100});
    target.clip.crop=c;
    syncCropPanel();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
    else if(target.kind==='background'&&typeof applyBackground==='function')applyBackground();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
  };
  window.ethvelleResetCrop=function(){const target=getCropTarget();if(!target)return;target.clip.crop={left:0,top:0,right:0,bottom:0};syncCropPanel();if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});};
  function cssCropTransform(clip,transform,sourceEl,motion){
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),sw=Math.max(1,stage?stage.clientWidth:1),sh=Math.max(1,stage?stage.clientHeight:1);
    const iw=Math.max(1,sourceEl&&(sourceEl.videoWidth||sourceEl.naturalWidth)||sw),ih=Math.max(1,sourceEl&&(sourceEl.videoHeight||sourceEl.naturalHeight)||sh);
    const c=cropFractions(clip.crop),cropW=iw*(1-c.left-c.right),cropH=ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom);
    const baseContain=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),hasCrop=(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom)>0.0001;
    const desiredScale=hasCrop?Math.max(sw/cropW,sh/cropH):baseContain;
    const scaleFactor=desiredScale/Math.max(.0001,baseContain);
    const cropDx=(c.right-c.left)*iw*desiredScale/2,cropDy=(c.bottom-c.top)*ih*desiredScale/2;
    const tr=transform||clip.transform||{},m=motion||{};
    const ox=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(m.x)||0)+cropDx,oy=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(m.y)||0)+cropDy;
    const rotation=(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(m.rotation)||0),scale=(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(m.scale)||1)*scaleFactor;
    return `translate(${ox}px,${oy}px) rotate(${rotation}deg) scale(${scale}) scaleX(${clip.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${clip.mirrorY?-1:1})`;
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=function(){
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):0);
    const id=getSelectedId();
    (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(ov=>{
      if(ov.type!=='video'&&ov.type!=='image')return;
      const start=Number(ov.trimStart)||0,end=Number(ov.trimEnd)||start;
      if(now<start-.001||now>=end+.001)return;
      const c=cropFractions(ov.crop);
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom<=.0001)return;
      const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;if(!el)return;
      const tracked=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,now):ov.transform;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',now):null;
      const tr=ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId?(tracked||ov.transform):(keyed||tracked||ov.transform);
      const motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
      const value=cssCropTransform(ov,tr,el,motion)+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'')
      if(el.style.transform!==value)el.style.transform=value;
      const chroma=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)?overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id]:null;
      if(chroma&&chroma.style.transform!==value)chroma.style.transform=value;
    });
    const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(now):bgFor(id);
    const img=$('bgImgLayer');
    if(bg&&img&&img.style.display!=='none'){
      const c=cropFractions(bg.crop);
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom>.0001){
        const tr=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(bg,'transform',now)||bg.transform):bg.transform;
        const value=cssCropTransform(bg,tr,img,null);if(img.style.transform!==value)img.style.transform=value;
      }
    }
  };
  const oldRenderOverlay=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldRenderOverlay==='function'&&!oldRenderOverlay.__evv87Crop){
    window.renderOverlayMedia=function(){const r=oldRenderOverlay.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.renderOverlayMedia.__evv87Crop=true;
  }
  const oldApplyBg=window.applyBackground;
  if(typeof oldApplyBg==='function'&&!oldApplyBg.__evv87Crop){
    window.applyBackground=function(){const r=oldApplyBg.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.applyBackground.__evv87Crop=true;
  }
  const oldApplyBgT=window.applyBgTransform;
  if(typeof oldApplyBgT==='function'&&!oldApplyBgT.__evv87Crop){
    window.applyBgTransform=function(){const r=oldApplyBgT.apply(this,arguments);window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview();return r;};
    window.applyBgTransform.__evv87Crop=true;
  }

  // Reconcile crop tool visibility and the active selection without scanning all timeline rows.
  const oldSelect3=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect3==='function'&&!oldSelect3.__evv87Crop){window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const r=oldSelect3.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncMediaTools);return r;};window.selectClipForChrome.__evv87Crop=true;}
  ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();},{once:true});
  else requestAnimationFrame(()=>{ensureMediaTools();syncMediaTools();});
})();
</script>

     responsive transform sheet and visual on-canvas crop tool. -->

<script id="ethvelle-v89-deep-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Prevent a duplicate crop-preview pass when applyBackground and renderOverlayMedia are
  // called back-to-back in the same frame. The actual video/image decoder work remains
  // independently throttled by the existing compositor.
  let cropPreviewLastAt=0,cropPreviewLastTime=NaN;
  const oldCropPreview=window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview;
  window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview=function(){
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const stamp=performance.now();
    if(Number.isFinite(cropPreviewLastTime)&&Math.abs(now-cropPreviewLastTime)<0.00001&&stamp-cropPreviewLastAt<5)return;
    cropPreviewLastAt=stamp;cropPreviewLastTime=now;
    const editingId=window.__ethvelleCropEditingId==null?'':String(window.__ethvelleCropEditingId);
    const map=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls?overlayMediaEls:{};
    const list=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
    for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){
      const ov=list[i];
      if(!ov||(ov.type!=='video'&&ov.type!=='image'))continue;
      const id=String(ov.id),el=map[id];if(!el||id===editingId)continue;
      const c=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(ov.crop):{left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom<=0.0001)continue;
      const tracked=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,now):ov.transform;
      const keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',now):null;
      const tr=ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId?(tracked||ov.transform):(keyed||tracked||ov.transform);
      const iw=Math.max(1,el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth||1),ih=Math.max(1,el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight||1);
      const sw=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientWidth||1),sh=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientHeight||1);
      const cropW=iw*(1-c.left-c.right),cropH=ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom),base=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih);
      const desired=Math.max(sw/cropW,sh/cropH),factor=desired/Math.max(.0001,base);
      const dx=(c.right-c.left)*iw*desired/2,dy=(c.bottom-c.top)*ih*desired/2;
      const motion=typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
      const value=`translate(${(Number(tr?.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion?.x)||0)+dx}px,${(Number(tr?.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion?.y)||0)+dy}px) rotate(${(Number(tr?.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion?.rotation)||0)}deg) scale(${(Number(tr?.scale)||1)*(Number(motion?.scale)||1)*factor}) scaleX(${ov.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${ov.mirrorY?-1:1})`+(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(ov):'');
      if(el.style.transform!==value)el.style.transform=value;
      const chroma=typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases?overlayChromaCanvases[id]:null;
      if(chroma&&chroma.style.transform!==value)chroma.style.transform=value;
    }
    const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(now):null;
    const img=$('bgImgLayer');
    if(bg&&img&&img.style.display!=='none'&&String(bg.id)!==editingId){
      const c=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(bg.crop):{left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
      if(c.left+c.right+c.top+c.bottom>0.0001){
        const tr=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(bg,'transform',now)||bg.transform):bg.transform;
        const iw=Math.max(1,img.naturalWidth||1),ih=Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight||1),sw=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientWidth||1),sh=Math.max(1,$('canvasStage')?.clientHeight||1);
        const desired=Math.max(sw/(iw*(1-c.left-c.right)),sh/(ih*(1-c.top-c.bottom))),base=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),factor=desired/Math.max(.0001,base);
        const value=`translate(${(Number(tr?.offsetX)||0)+(c.right-c.left)*iw*desired/2}px,${(Number(tr?.offsetY)||0)+(c.bottom-c.top)*ih*desired/2}px) rotate(${Number(tr?.rotation)||0}deg) scale(${(Number(tr?.scale)||1)*factor})`;
        if(img.style.transform!==value)img.style.transform=value;
      }
    }
  };

  // Transform panel: fit its visible top edge immediately below the transport row.
  // The sheet keeps its natural content height until the available space is exhausted.
  function fitTransformPanel(){
    const editor=$('screen-editor'),panel=$('toolPanel'),row=editor?.querySelector('.transport-row');
    if(!editor||!panel||!row||!panel.classList.contains('open'))return;
    const er=editor.getBoundingClientRect(),rr=row.getBoundingClientRect();
    const available=Math.max(150,Math.floor(er.bottom-rr.bottom-8));
    const header=panel.querySelector('.tool-panel-header');
    const headerH=header?header.getBoundingClientRect().height:48;
    panel.classList.add('ev-v89-fit-panel');
    panel.style.maxHeight=available+'px';
    const body=$('panelBody');if(body)body.style.maxHeight=Math.max(80,available-headerH-4)+'px';
  }
  const prevOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof prevOpen==='function'&&!prevOpen.__evv89Panel){
    const wrapped=function(key){
      const result=prevOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanel));
      else{const p=$('toolPanel');if(p){p.classList.remove('ev-v89-fit-panel');p.style.maxHeight='';const b=$('panelBody');if(b)b.style.maxHeight='';}}
      if(key!=='crop'&&window.__ethvelleCropEditingId)hideCropEditor(false);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv89Panel=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if($('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('ev-v89-fit-panel'))requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanel);},{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if($('toolPanel')?.classList.contains('ev-v89-fit-panel'))requestAnimationFrame(fitTransformPanel);},{passive:true});

  // The Crop tool is a real on-canvas frame. Crop fractions are stored on the selected
  // clip, using the same normalized model already consumed by preview and export.
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,Number(v)||0));
  const cropTarget=()=>{
    const id=typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':String(selectedClip);
    const clip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
    if(!clip)return null;
    if((clip.type==='image'||clip.type==='video')&&typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&overlays.includes(clip))return {id,clip,kind:'overlay',el:typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'?overlayMediaEls[id]:null};
    if(typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)&&backgrounds.includes(clip))return {id,clip,kind:'background',el:$('bgImgLayer')};
    return null;
  };
  const getCrop=clip=>{
    const c=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(clip?.crop):{left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    return {left:c.left||0,right:c.right||0,top:c.top||0,bottom:c.bottom||0};
  };
  let cropRoot=null,cropFrame=null,cropRatio='free',cropDrag=null,cropResizeObserver=null,cropOpen=false;
  function metrics(target){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return null;
    const sw=Math.max(1,stage.clientWidth),sh=Math.max(1,stage.clientHeight),el=target.el;
    const iw=Math.max(1,el&&(el.videoWidth||el.naturalWidth)||Number(target.clip.sourceWidth)||sw);
    const ih=Math.max(1,el&&(el.videoHeight||el.naturalHeight)||Number(target.clip.sourceHeight)||sh);
    const scale=Math.min(sw/iw,sh/ih),w=iw*scale,h=ih*scale;
    return {sw,sh,iw,ih,x:(sw-w)/2,y:(sh-h)/2,w,h,aspect:iw/ih};
  }
  function liveTransform(clip){
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const base=typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function'?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip):(window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue?.(clip,'transform',now)||clip.transform||{});
    const key=window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue?.(clip,'transform',now)||base||clip.transform||{};
    const isBackground=typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)&&backgrounds.includes(clip);
    const motion=!isBackground&&typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,now):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
    return `translate(${(Number(key.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion?.x)||0)}px,${(Number(key.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion?.y)||0)}px) rotate(${(Number(key.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion?.rotation)||0)}deg) scale(${(Number(key.scale)||1)*(Number(motion?.scale)||1)})`;
  }
  function ensureCropRoot(){
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(!stage)return;
    if(cropRoot&&cropRoot.isConnected)return;
    cropRoot=document.createElement('div');cropRoot.className='ev-v89-crop-root';cropRoot.id='evV89CropRoot';cropRoot.style.display='none';
    cropFrame=document.createElement('div');cropFrame.className='ev-v89-crop-frame';cropFrame.id='evV89CropFrame';
    ['nw','n','ne','e','se','s','sw','w'].forEach(dir=>{const h=document.createElement('span');h.className='ev-v89-crop-handle';h.dataset.handle=dir;cropFrame.appendChild(h);});
    cropRoot.appendChild(cropFrame);stage.appendChild(cropRoot);
  }
  function paintCropFrame(){
    if(!cropOpen)return;
    const target=cropTarget(),stage=$('canvasStage');if(!target||!stage)return;
    ensureCropRoot();const m=metrics(target);if(!m)return;
    const c=getCrop(target.clip),w=m.w*(1-c.left-c.right),h=m.h*(1-c.top-c.bottom);
    cropRoot.style.display='block';cropRoot.style.transform=liveTransform(target.clip);
    cropFrame.style.left=(m.x+c.left*m.w)+'px';cropFrame.style.top=(m.y+c.top*m.h)+'px';
    cropFrame.style.width=Math.max(18,w)+'px';cropFrame.style.height=Math.max(18,h)+'px';
    // Show the un-cropped source while editing. The final crop transform is restored on exit.
    if(target.el)target.el.style.transform=liveTransform(target.clip);
  }
  function updateRatioButtons(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.ev-v89-ratio').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.ratio===cropRatio));
  }
  function cropPanelBody(){
    return `<div class="ev-v89-crop-panel">
      <div class="ev-v89-ratio-label">Aspect ratio</div>
      <div class="ev-v89-ratios">
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio active" data-ratio="free" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('free')">Free</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="1:1" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('1:1')">1:1</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="16:9" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('16:9')">16:9</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="9:16" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('9:16')">9:16</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="4:3" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('4:3')">4:3</button>
        <button type="button" class="ev-v89-ratio" data-ratio="3:4" onclick="ethvelleV89SetCropRatio('3:4')">3:4</button>
      </div>
      <div class="ev-v89-crop-actions"><button type="button" class="ev-v89-crop-reset" onclick="ethvelleV89ResetCrop()">Reset crop</button><button type="button" class="ev-v89-crop-done" onclick="closePanel()">Apply crop</button></div>
    </div>`;
  }
  if(typeof panelContent!=='undefined')panelContent.crop={title:'Crop',body:cropPanelBody()};
  function showCropEditor(){
    const target=cropTarget();if(!target){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Select an image or video layer to crop.',{icon:window.toastIcons?.info});return;}
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying&&typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    // Reset any stale crop state from a previous crop session on this clip.
    cropOpen=false;cropDrag=null;cropRatio='free';
    if(cropRoot){cropRoot.style.display='none';try{cropRoot.remove();}catch(_e){}cropRoot=null;cropFrame=null;}
    cropOpen=true;window.__ethvelleCropEditingId=target.id;
    // Invalidate the clip-path cache for this element so a fresh clip-path is computed on Apply.
    if(typeof window.ethvelleApplyCropPreview==='function'&&target.el){
      try{const stage=$('canvasStage');if(stage&&target.el.style.clipPath&&target.el.style.clipPath!=='none'){target.el.style.clipPath='none';target.el.style.webkitClipPath='none';}}catch(_e){}
    }
    if(target.el){target.el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>requestAnimationFrame(paintCropFrame),{once:true});target.el.addEventListener('load',()=>requestAnimationFrame(paintCropFrame),{once:true});}
    const screen=$('screen-editor');if(screen)screen.classList.add('ev-v89-crop-active');
    paintCropFrame();updateRatioButtons();
    if(cropResizeObserver)cropResizeObserver.disconnect();
    const stage=$('canvasStage');if(stage&&typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined'){cropResizeObserver=new ResizeObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(paintCropFrame));cropResizeObserver.observe(stage);}
  }
  function hideCropEditor(apply){
    if(!cropOpen&&!cropRoot)return;
    const target=cropTarget();cropOpen=false;cropDrag=null;
    if(cropResizeObserver){cropResizeObserver.disconnect();cropResizeObserver=null;}
    $('screen-editor')?.classList.remove('ev-v89-crop-active');
    if(cropRoot){cropRoot.style.display='none';cropRoot.remove();cropRoot=null;cropFrame=null;}
    delete window.__ethvelleCropEditingId;
    if(apply&&target){if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}
  }
  window.ethvelleV89SetCropRatio=function(value){
    const target=cropTarget();if(!target)return;
    cropRatio=String(value||'free');
    if(cropRatio!=='free'){
      const m=metrics(target);if(!m)return;
      const parts=cropRatio.split(':').map(Number),wanted=parts[0]/parts[1],source=m.aspect;
      let wf=1,hf=1;
      if(source>wanted)wf=wanted/source;else hf=source/wanted;
      const x=(1-wf)/2,y=(1-hf)/2;
      target.clip.crop={left:x,right:x,top:y,bottom:y};
    }
    updateRatioButtons();paintCropFrame();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
  };
  window.ethvelleV89ResetCrop=function(){
    const target=cropTarget();if(!target)return;
    target.clip.crop={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};cropRatio='free';updateRatioButtons();paintCropFrame();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
  };
  function setCropFractions(target,c){
    const clampPair=(a,b)=>{a=clamp(a,0,.92);b=clamp(b,0,.92);const sum=a+b;if(sum>.92){const f=.92/sum;a*=f;b*=f;}return [a,b];};
    [c.left,c.right]=clampPair(c.left,c.right);[c.top,c.bottom]=clampPair(c.top,c.bottom);
    target.clip.crop={left:c.left,right:c.right,top:c.top,bottom:c.bottom};
  }
  function beginCropDrag(e){
    const target=cropTarget(),m=target&&metrics(target);if(!target||!m)return;
    const handle=e.target.closest('.ev-v89-crop-handle')?.dataset.handle||'move';
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const tr=window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue?.(target.clip,'transform',now)||target.clip.transform||{};
    const isBg=target.kind==='background';
    const motion=!isBg&&typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function'?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(target.clip,now):{scale:1,rotation:0};
    const layerScale=Math.max(.0001,(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion?.scale)||1));
    const layerRotation=((Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion?.rotation)||0))*Math.PI/180;
    const stage=$('canvasStage'),stageRect=stage&&stage.getBoundingClientRect();
    const sx=stageRect&&stageRect.width?stage.clientWidth/stageRect.width:1;
    const sy=stageRect&&stageRect.height?stage.clientHeight/stageRect.height:1;
    // Capture on the stable frame, not on a handle whose position changes during resizing.
    // Invert the actual computed crop-root matrix so zoom, layer scale and layer rotation all
    // convert the finger delta into the same local coordinate space as the crop fractions.
    let inverseMatrix=null;
    try{
      const Matrix=window.DOMMatrixReadOnly||window.DOMMatrix;
      const cssTransform=cropRoot&&getComputedStyle(cropRoot).transform;
      if(Matrix&&cssTransform&&cssTransform!=='none'){
        const inv=new Matrix(cssTransform).inverse();
        inverseMatrix={a:inv.a,b:inv.b,c:inv.c,d:inv.d};
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    const capture=cropFrame;
    cropDrag={pointerId:e.pointerId,handle,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,m,c:getCrop(target.clip),
      invScale:1/layerScale,cos:Math.cos(layerRotation),sin:Math.sin(layerRotation),inverseMatrix,stageScaleX:sx,stageScaleY:sy,capture};
    try{cropDrag.capture.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }
  function moveCropDrag(e){
    if(!cropDrag||e.pointerId!==cropDrag.pointerId)return;
    const target=cropTarget();if(!target)return;
    const d=cropDrag;
    // getBoundingClientRect() includes the canvas zoom scale; convert finger movement back
    // into stage CSS pixels before applying the selected layer's inverse scale/rotation.
    const screenX=(e.clientX-d.startX)*d.stageScaleX;
    const screenY=(e.clientY-d.startY)*d.stageScaleY;
    const localX=d.inverseMatrix?(d.inverseMatrix.a*screenX+d.inverseMatrix.c*screenY):((screenX*d.cos+screenY*d.sin)*d.invScale);
    const localY=d.inverseMatrix?(d.inverseMatrix.b*screenX+d.inverseMatrix.d*screenY):((-screenX*d.sin+screenY*d.cos)*d.invScale);
    const dx=localX/Math.max(1,d.m.w),dy=localY/Math.max(1,d.m.h),c={...d.c},h=d.handle;
    const w0=1-d.c.left-d.c.right,h0=1-d.c.top-d.c.bottom;
    if(h==='move'){
      const maxL=1-d.c.right-w0; // remains the original left unless clamped at the image edge
      c.left=clamp(d.c.left+dx,0,1-w0);c.right=1-w0-c.left;
      c.top=clamp(d.c.top+dy,0,1-h0);c.bottom=1-h0-c.top;
    }else if(cropRatio==='free'){
      if(h.includes('w'))c.left=clamp(d.c.left+dx,0,1-d.c.right-.08);
      if(h.includes('e'))c.right=clamp(d.c.right-dx,0,1-d.c.left-.08);
      if(h.includes('n'))c.top=clamp(d.c.top+dy,0,1-d.c.bottom-.08);
      if(h.includes('s'))c.bottom=clamp(d.c.bottom-dy,0,1-d.c.top-.08);
    }else{
      const p=cropRatio.split(':').map(Number),ratio=(p[0]/p[1])/d.m.aspect;
      if(h==='e'||h==='w'){
        let w=clamp(w0+(h==='e'?dx:-dx),.08,1);
        let hh=clamp(w/ratio,.08,1);w=hh*ratio;
        if(h==='e')c.right=1-d.c.left-w;else c.left=1-d.c.right-w;
        c.top=(1-hh)/2;c.bottom=1-hh-c.top;
      }else if(h==='n'||h==='s'){
        let hh=clamp(h0+(h==='s'?dy:-dy),.08,1),w=clamp(hh*ratio,.08,1);hh=w/ratio;
        if(h==='s')c.bottom=1-d.c.top-hh;else c.top=1-d.c.bottom-hh;
        c.left=(1-w)/2;c.right=1-w-c.left;
      }else{
        const dirX=h.includes('w')?-1:1,dirY=h.includes('n')?-1:1;
        const candidateW=clamp(w0+dirX*dx,.08,1),candidateH=clamp(h0+dirY*dy,.08,1);
        let w=Math.abs(candidateW-w0)/Math.max(.08,w0)>=Math.abs(candidateH-h0)/Math.max(.08,h0)?candidateW:candidateH*ratio;
        let hh=w/ratio;if(hh>.92){hh=.92;w=hh*ratio;}if(w>.92){w=.92;hh=w/ratio;}
        if(dirX<0)c.left=1-d.c.right-w;else c.right=1-d.c.left-w;
        if(dirY<0)c.top=1-d.c.bottom-hh;else c.bottom=1-d.c.top-hh;
      }
    }
    setCropFractions(target,c);paintCropFrame();e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  }
  function endCropDrag(e){
    if(!cropDrag||e.pointerId!==undefined&&e.pointerId!==cropDrag.pointerId)return;
    const capture=cropDrag.capture;cropDrag=null;
    try{if(e.pointerId!==undefined&&capture&&capture.hasPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId))capture.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});
  }
  /* Capture the crop gesture before timeline/canvas handlers. This is especially important
     on Android WebView, where transformed canvas layers can otherwise take pointer capture. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    if(!cropOpen||!cropFrame||!e.target?.closest?.('.ev-v89-crop-frame'))return;
    beginCropDrag(e);
    if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(cropDrag&&e.pointerId===cropDrag.pointerId)moveCropDrag(e);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(cropDrag&&e.pointerId===cropDrag.pointerId)endCropDrag(e);},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(cropDrag&&e.pointerId===cropDrag.pointerId)endCropDrag(e);},true);
  const prevOpenPanel=window.openPanel;
  // This wrapper is installed after the Transform-panel wrapper above, so both remain active.
  if(typeof prevOpenPanel==='function'&&!prevOpenPanel.__evv89Crop){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=prevOpenPanel.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='crop')requestAnimationFrame(()=>showCropEditor());else if(cropOpen)hideCropEditor(false);return result;};
    wrapped.__evv89Crop=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  const prevClose=window.closePanel;
  if(typeof prevClose==='function'&&!prevClose.__evv89Crop){
    const wrapped=function(){const wasCrop=cropOpen;const result=prevClose.apply(this,arguments);if(wasCrop)hideCropEditor(true);return result;};
    wrapped.__evv89Crop=true;window.closePanel=wrapped;
  }
  // Crop preview is temporarily disabled only for the actively-edited layer; other cropped
  // media continue to render normally. On Apply, the crop is committed to the existing
  // preview/export crop data model without recreating media elements.
  ensureCropRoot();
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',()=>{if(cropOpen)requestAnimationFrame(paintCropFrame);},{passive:true});
})();
</script>

     but must never participate in the editor's flex sizing or move the preview canvas. */

    position:fixed!important;left:0!important;right:0!important;bottom:0!important;top:auto!important;

<!-- Ethvelle v91: reliable transform ruler gestures, playhead-key selection sync,
     low-latency bottom-toolbar scrolling, stable crop drag in transformed space,
     and right-anchored Key/Zoom controls. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v91-interaction-css">
  /* Transform ruler inputs are deliberately driven by the entire ruler, not the hidden
     thumb-only global slider overlay. The visible tick artwork remains unchanged. */
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler{touch-action:none!important;user-select:none!important;-webkit-user-select:none!important;}
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler input[type="range"]{
    pointer-events:none!important;opacity:0!important;touch-action:none!important;
  }
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler::after{background:#2F5CE8!important;box-shadow:none!important;}
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler.evtr-dragging{cursor:ew-resize!important;}
  #toolPanel #evltAdjustmentsPanel .evlt-done,
  #toolPanel #evltAdjustmentsPanel .evlt-apply,

  #toolPanel #evltAdjustmentsPanel [data-action="apply"],
  #toolPanel #evltAdjustmentsPanel .evlt-confirm,
  #toolPanel #evltAdjustmentsPanel .evlt-apply{display:none!important;}

  /* Contextual bottom tools: keep native tap actions, but use a compositor-friendly,
     requestAnimationFrame horizontal scroll path so the row follows the finger directly. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip .tool-item{touch-action:pan-y!important;flex-shrink:0!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip.ev-v91-toolbar-dragging{cursor:grabbing!important;}

  /* Keep Previous / Play / Next centered on the playhead; Key and Zoom live at the far
     right of the same row and no longer widen or displace the centre transport cluster. */
  #screen-editor > .transport-row > #transportKeyBtn{
    position:absolute!important;left:auto!important;right:56px!important;top:50%!important;
    bottom:auto!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;
    display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;
    width:28px!important;height:28px!important;min-width:28px!important;min-height:28px!important;
    z-index:8!important;margin:0!important;flex:0 0 28px!important;
  }
  #screen-editor > .transport-row > #transportZoomBtn{
    position:absolute!important;left:auto!important;right:10px!important;top:50%!important;
    bottom:auto!important;transform:translateY(-50%)!important;
    display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;
    width:28px!important;height:28px!important;min-width:28px!important;min-height:28px!important;
    z-index:8!important;margin:0!important;flex:0 0 28px!important;
  }
  @media(max-width:380px){
    #screen-editor > .transport-row > #transportKeyBtn{right:48px!important;}
    #screen-editor > .transport-row > #transportZoomBtn{right:6px!important;}
  }

  /* Crop handles gain a larger invisible touch target without changing their visible size,
     colour, border or position. */
  #canvasStage .ev-v89-crop-handle::after{
    content:""!important;display:block!important;position:absolute!important;
    left:-9px!important;right:-9px!important;top:-9px!important;bottom:-9px!important;
    border:0!important;background:transparent!important;box-shadow:none!important;
    pointer-events:auto!important;
  }
  #canvasStage .ev-v89-crop-frame,#canvasStage .ev-v89-crop-handle{touch-action:none!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v91-interaction-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* Put Key and Zoom beside the transport cluster, not inside it. The Previous / Play /
     Next cluster can then stay centered exactly on the blue playhead while these two tools
     sit at the far right edge of the same row. */
  function dockTransportUtilityButtons(){
    const row=document.querySelector('#screen-editor > .transport-row');if(!row)return;
    const key=$('transportKeyBtn'),zoom=$('transportZoomBtn');
    if(key&&key.parentElement!==row)row.appendChild(key);
    if(zoom&&zoom.parentElement!==row)row.appendChild(zoom);
  }
  dockTransportUtilityButtons();
  requestAnimationFrame(dockTransportUtilityButtons);

  /* 1) Transform ruler: map any horizontal touch on the ruler to the range's value.
     This bypasses the global thumb-only slider hit area that made the ruler feel stuck. */
  function bindTransformRuler(ruler){
    if(!ruler||ruler.dataset.evV91Bound==='1')return;
    const input=ruler.querySelector('input[type="range"]');if(!input)return;
    ruler.dataset.evV91Bound='1';
    input.style.setProperty('pointer-events','none','important');
    input.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
    ruler.style.setProperty('touch-action','none','important');
    let drag=null,raf=0,pendingX=0;
    function setFromClientX(x,emit){
      if(!drag)return;
      const rect=ruler.getBoundingClientRect();if(!rect.width)return;
      const usable=Math.max(1,rect.width-20);
      const min=drag.min,max=drag.max,step=drag.step;
      /* The finger drags the grey ruler; the blue centre marker never moves. */
      let value=drag.startValue+((drag.startX-x)/usable)*(max-min);
      if(step>0)value=min+Math.round((value-min)/step)*step;
      value=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,value));
      const decimals=(String(input.step||'1').split('.')[1]||'').length;
      const next=String(Number(value.toFixed(Math.min(6,decimals))));
      if(String(input.value)!==next){input.value=next;input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));}
      if(emit)input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
    }
    function flush(){if(!drag)return;if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}setFromClientX(pendingX,false);}
    ruler.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
      if(e.isPrimary===false||(e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==0))return;
      const target=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('button'):null;if(target)return;
      const min=Number.isFinite(Number(input.min))?Number(input.min):0;
      const max=Number.isFinite(Number(input.max))?Number(input.max):100;
      drag={id:e.pointerId,startX:e.clientX,startValue:Number(input.value)||0,min,max,step:input.step==='any'?0:(Number(input.step)||1)};
      pendingX=e.clientX;
      try{ruler.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      ruler.classList.add('evtr-dragging');
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      if(!drag||drag.id!==e.pointerId)return;
      pendingX=e.clientX;
      if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{raf=0;if(drag)setFromClientX(pendingX,false);});
      if(e.cancelable)e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },{passive:false});
    const finish=e=>{
      if(!drag||e.pointerId!==drag.id)return;
      flush();input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));drag=null;
      ruler.classList.remove('evtr-dragging');
      try{ruler.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(_e){}
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    };
    ruler.addEventListener('pointerup',finish,{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('pointercancel',finish,{passive:false});
    ruler.addEventListener('lostpointercapture',()=>{drag=null;ruler.classList.remove('evtr-dragging');if(raf){cancelAnimationFrame(raf);raf=0;}},{passive:true});
  }
  function scanTransformRulers(root){
    if(!root)return;
    if(root.nodeType===1&&root.matches?.('.evtr-ruler'))bindTransformRuler(root);
    root.querySelectorAll?.('.evtr-ruler').forEach(bindTransformRuler);
  }
  scanTransformRulers(document);
  const panel=$('toolPanel');
  if(panel&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    new MutationObserver(records=>records.forEach(record=>record.addedNodes.forEach(node=>{if(node.nodeType===1)scanTransformRulers(node);}))).observe(panel,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv91Rulers){
    window.openPanel=function(key){const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>scanTransformRulers($('toolPanel')));return result;};
    window.openPanel.__evv91Rulers=true;
  }

  /* 2) RAF-driven bottom-tool scrolling. A normal tap is never cancelled, so tool buttons
     remain clickable. Only a clearly horizontal drag takes ownership of the gesture. */
  function bindBottomToolbar(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');
    if(!row)return;
    // Intentionally no JS pointer listeners: native overflow scrolling has momentum,
    // preserves clicks, and avoids a per-pointermove JavaScript scroll loop on Android.
    row.dataset.evV91Scroll='native';
  }
  bindBottomToolbar();

  /* 3) Playhead contact is actual keyframe selection, not only a temporary red highlight.
     The sync runs after the canonical clock/render updates and inspects only the active row. */
  function timelineTime(){
    try{if(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')return Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime());}catch(_e){}
    return Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
  }
  function setMarkerState(track,selector,activeIndex,contactIndex){
    if(!track)return;
    const markers=track.querySelectorAll('.kf-marker');
    markers.forEach(marker=>{
      const raw=marker.getAttribute(selector);
      const index=raw==null?-1:Number(raw);
      const active=index===activeIndex&&activeIndex>=0;
      const under=index===contactIndex&&contactIndex>=0;
      marker.classList.toggle('active',active);
      marker.classList.toggle('under-playhead',under);
    });
  }
  function nearestKeyIndex(keys,property,t,tolerance){
    let best=-1,bestDistance=Infinity;
    for(let i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
      const key=keys[i];if(!key||(property&&key.property!==property))continue;
      const d=Math.abs(Number(key.t)-t);
      if(Number.isFinite(d)&&d<=tolerance&&d<bestDistance){best=i;bestDistance=d;}
    }
    return best;
  }
  function syncPlayheadKeySelection(){
    const id=(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null)?'':String(selectedClip);
    const t=timelineTime();if(!Number.isFinite(t))return;
    if(!id){
      const hadChar=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
      if(hadChar){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      return;
    }
    const pps=typeof pxPerSecond==='function'?Math.max(1,Number(pxPerSecond())||1):100;
    const objectClip=typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;
    const isCharacter=(id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id));
    const isCharacterLayer=/^char\d+$/.test(id);
    if(objectClip){
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
      const property=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeProperty(objectClip):(objectClip.type==='audio'?'volume':'transform');
      const keys=Array.isArray(objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes)?objectClip.ethvelleMediaKeyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.ethvelleObjectKeyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,property,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
      if(idx>=0){
        if(!chosen||String(chosen.id)!==id||Number(chosen.index)!==idx)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe={id,index:idx,source:'playhead'};
      }else if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)===id){window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;}
      let track=window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack;
      if(!track||!track.isConnected||String(track.dataset.objectKfTrack||'')!==id)track=document.querySelector(`[data-object-kf-track="${id.replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/"/g,'\\"')}"]`);
      if(track){window.__ethvelleActiveObjectKeyframeTrack=track;const selected=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;const active=selected&&String(selected.id)===id?Number(selected.index):-1;setMarkerState(track,'data-object-kf-idx',active,idx);}
      return;
    }
    if(isCharacterLayer){
      /* A character ROW selection still represents its active main animation clip. */
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      const keys=activeClip&&Array.isArray(activeClip.keyframes)?activeClip.keyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.keyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.keyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,null,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const previous=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'?selectedKfIdx:null;
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined')selectedKfIdx=idx>=0?idx:null;
      setMarkerState($('kfTrack'),'data-kf-idx',idx,idx);
      if((previous==null?-1:Number(previous))!==idx&&typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
      return;
    }
    if(isCharacter){
      window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
      const clip=typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(id):null;
      const keys=clip&&Array.isArray(clip.keyframes)?clip.keyframes:[];
      const tolerance=typeof window.keyframeContactWindow==='function'?Number(window.keyframeContactWindow()):Math.max(.05,9/pps);
      const idx=nearestKeyIndex(keys,null,t,Math.max(.001,tolerance));
      const previous=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'?selectedKfIdx:null;
      if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined')selectedKfIdx=idx>=0?idx:null;
      const track=$(id==='main'?'kfTrack':'kfTrack-'+id);
      setMarkerState(track,'data-kf-idx',idx,idx);
      if((previous==null?-1:Number(previous))!==idx&&typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();
      return;
    }
    const chosen=window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe;
    if(chosen&&String(chosen.id)!==id)window.__ethvelleSelectedObjectKeyframe=null;
    if(typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null){selectedKfIdx=null;if(typeof syncBottomToolsMode==='function')syncBottomToolsMode();}
  }
  let keySyncRaf=0;
  function scheduleKeySync(){if(keySyncRaf)return;keySyncRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{keySyncRaf=0;try{syncPlayheadKeySelection();}catch(err){console.warn('Keyframe contact sync:',err);}});}
  const oldSelectForKeySync=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelectForKeySync==='function'&&!oldSelectForKeySync.__evv91KeySync){
    window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const result=oldSelectForKeySync.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.selectClipForChrome.__evv91KeySync=true;
  }
  const oldUpdateKey=window.updateKeyButtonState;
  if(typeof oldUpdateKey==='function'&&!oldUpdateKey.__evv91Contact){
    window.updateKeyButtonState=function(){const result=oldUpdateKey.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.updateKeyButtonState.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldSetTime=window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime;
  if(typeof oldSetTime==='function'&&!oldSetTime.__evv91Contact){
    window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime=function(){const result=oldSetTime.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldSeek=window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds;
  if(typeof oldSeek==='function'&&!oldSeek.__evv91Contact){
    window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds=function(){const result=oldSeek.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldMovePlayhead=window.movePlayhead;
  if(typeof oldMovePlayhead==='function'&&!oldMovePlayhead.__evv91Contact){
    window.movePlayhead=function(){const result=oldMovePlayhead.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.movePlayhead.__evv91Contact=true;
  }
  const oldRender=window.renderFrame;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'&&!oldRender.__evv91Contact){
    window.renderFrame=function(){const result=oldRender.apply(this,arguments);scheduleKeySync();return result;};
    window.renderFrame.__evv91Contact=true;
  }

  /* A direct pointer selection is more reliable on Android than depending on the synthesized
     click after pointer capture / timeline scrolling. The click is suppressed only for that
     same marker so the old inline handler never selects it twice. */
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(!marker||e.isPrimary===false)return;
    const objectTrack=marker.closest('.object-keyframe-track');
    const rawObject=marker.getAttribute('data-object-kf-idx');
    const rawChar=marker.getAttribute('data-kf-idx');
    marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt=String(Date.now());
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    try{
      if(objectTrack&&rawObject!=null){
        const id=String(objectTrack.dataset.objectKfTrack||'');
        if(id&&typeof window.selectObjectKeyframe==='function')window.selectObjectKeyframe(id,Number(rawObject));
      }else if(rawChar!=null){
        const clipEl=marker.closest('.anim-clip');
        const ref=clipEl&&clipEl.id==='animClipEl'?'main':clipEl&&clipEl.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+clipEl.dataset.segIdx:'';
        if(ref&&typeof window.selectKeyframe==='function')window.selectKeyframe(Number(rawChar),ref);
      }
    }catch(err){console.warn('Keyframe pointer selection:',err);}
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    const marker=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('#timelineInner .kf-marker'):null;
    if(!marker)return;
    const stamp=Number(marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt)||0;
    if(stamp&&Date.now()-stamp<900){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();delete marker.dataset.evV91PointerAt;}
  },true);

  /* 4) Reverse is restricted to real video overlays. Backgrounds, images, text and other
     normal layers never receive the Reverse action. Adjustment sliders auto-apply their
     CSS filter live; the panel deliberately has no confirmation/check action. */
  function enforceVideoReverseVisibility(){
    const reverse=$('clipReverseTool');if(!reverse)return;
    const id=String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'?'':(selectedClip||''));
    const clip=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays.find(o=>String(o.id)===id):null;
    reverse.style.display=clip&&clip.type==='video'?'flex':'none';
  }
  const oldSyncTools=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSyncTools==='function'&&!oldSyncTools.__evv91VideoReverse){
    window.syncBottomToolsMode=function(){const result=oldSyncTools.apply(this,arguments);enforceVideoReverseVisibility();return result;};
    window.syncBottomToolsMode.__evv91VideoReverse=true;
  }
  const oldSelectClip=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelectClip==='function'&&!oldSelectClip.__evv91VideoReverse){
    window.selectClipForChrome=function(){const result=oldSelectClip.apply(this,arguments);enforceVideoReverseVisibility();return result;};
    window.selectClipForChrome.__evv91VideoReverse=true;
  }
  enforceVideoReverseVisibility();
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&$('bottomToolsClip'))new MutationObserver(enforceVideoReverseVisibility).observe($('bottomToolsClip'),{childList:true,subtree:true});
  document.addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.matches('#evAdj_brightness,#evAdj_contrast,#evAdj_saturation,#evAdj_hue,#evAdj_blur,#evAdj_grayscale,#evAdj_sepia')){clearTimeout(window.__ethvelleAdjustmentPersistTimer);if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}},true);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v93-transform-compat-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  // Ensure legacy visual overlays resolve to their own transform data before the v84
  // Transform bridge reads the active target. Non-media/character behavior remains untouched.
  const priorTarget=window.transformTarget;
  if(typeof priorTarget==='function'&&!priorTarget.__evv93MediaTarget){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const id=(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null)?String(selectedClip):'';
      const bgList=typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[];
      const ovList=typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[];
      if(id.startsWith('bg')){
        const bg=bgList.find(x=>String(x.id)===id);
        if(bg){if(!bg.transform)bg.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};return {obj:bg.transform,kind:'background',bgClip:bg};}
      }
      if(id){
        const ov=ovList.find(x=>String(x.id)===id);
        if(ov&&(ov.type==='image'||ov.type==='video')){
          if(!ov.transform)ov.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0};
          return {obj:ov.transform,kind:'overlay',ov};
        }
      }
      return priorTarget.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evv93MediaTarget=true;window.transformTarget=wrapped;
  }
  // Ruler values are already changed by the v91 pointer handler before it dispatches input.
  // Re-sync the ruler's tick-strip offset after the selected media transform is applied.
  const priorUpdate=window.updateTransform;
  if(typeof priorUpdate==='function'&&!priorUpdate.__evv93Apply){
    const wrapped=function(){
      // The v84 media bridge already writes the selected clip's live/keyframed transform
      // and repaints that one media layer. Do not repaint it a second time here (which
      // becomes expensive while dragging the ruler on a phone).
      const result=priorUpdate.apply(this,arguments);
      try{window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers?.();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv93Apply=true;window.updateTransform=wrapped;
  }
  // Refresh the transform inputs after opening the panel (including a restored legacy clip).
  const priorOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpen==='function'&&!priorOpen.__evv93TransformOpen){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=priorOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='transform')requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.syncTransformSliders?.();window.ethvelleSyncTransformRulers?.();}catch(_e){}});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv93TransformOpen=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v94-final-sync-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  // Re-render only the overlay list when the sheet is opened; video thumbnails are generated
  // lazily by renderOverlayListPanel() and the first stored frame is always used in its tile.
  const oldOpen=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv94OverlayThumbs){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(key==='overlay')requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.renderOverlayListPanel?.();}catch(_e){}});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv94OverlayThumbs=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>


<!-- Ethvelle v96: remove competing toolbar pointer-scroll driver; preserve ruler frame while shortening tick marks. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v95-crop-and-latency-css">
  /* The Transform panel stays the same size. Only the gray vertical ruler marks become half-height. */
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler{
    width:100%!important;max-width:100%!important;height:78px!important;
  }
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler::before{
    top:24px!important;bottom:24px!important;
  }
  #toolPanel .evtr-ruler::after{
    top:14px!important;bottom:14px!important;left:50%!important;
    transform:translateX(-50%)!important;
  }
  /* The requested Transform reset control is removed; Crop's separate Reset crop remains untouched. */
  #toolPanel .evtr-root > .evtr-footer{display:none!important;}

  /* A horizontal toolbar drag is handled immediately by touchmove below; leave vertical gestures to the page. */
  
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault::-webkit-scrollbar,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip::-webkit-scrollbar,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;}
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsKeyframe > .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsDefault .tool-item,
  #screen-editor #bottomToolsClip .tool-item,

  }

  /* Crop frame and its blue square handles must win hit-testing above the media/canvas layers. */
  #canvasStage #evV89CropRoot.ev-v89-crop-root{
    z-index:2147483000!important;pointer-events:none!important;touch-action:none!important;
  }
